# Unfilter digest 08 — pregnancy, birth and the first weeks

Read on 2026-11-17. Inputs: `voice/reddit-ur17.jsonl` (353 quotes), `voice/reddit-ur22.jsonl` (324), `voice/reddit-ur28.jsonl` (435), `voice/reddit-ur35.jsonl` (374), their four ledgers’ “What these posts add” sections, the build README and the current findings. Total: **1,486 quotes**.

This is a deliberately selected rereading share of previously excluded material. Every count here is a count of quote records, never people or prevalence. Several excerpts can come from one account; replies, crossposts and same-author follow-ups are not independent corroboration. Medical, policy, causal and legal statements below remain the authors’ reports, beliefs or questions. They have not been externally checked. No network or git was used.

The current findings already restored consent, suffering, difficult recovery, care navigation and reassurance loops. This share mostly specifies those mechanisms and widens the reader’s circumstances; it does not establish that those broad themes were wholly absent. The most consequential change is to stop assuming that pregnancy means a settled decision to continue and parent, that a partner means usable support, or that knowing whom to call makes care available. Planning still matters, but illness, threats, access rules and service capacity can defeat a carefully made plan.

The evidence below is deliberately extensive. Complete quote fields are reproduced without trimming, cleanup or punctuation changes. Other relevant records are listed as further supporting IDs. A record can support several patterns; a full quotation generally appears once, under its most specific pattern. Repeated IDs in a later support list refer back to that quotation. Money and Requests are indexes into the same evidence, with any missing full quotation supplied there. Current conclusions are quoted without importing their old corpus IDs.

## Patterns and implications for the current findings

### 1. Loss can interrupt the pregnancy timeline and create an immediate need for management choices, pain preparation, follow-up and a way to grieve.

**Belongs in:** §§2, 4, 5, 6, 9; add a loss and care-after-loss subsection.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The previous pregnancy is the yardstick and the previous birth sets the fear.”

**What should change or be retained:** Add actual miscarriage, ectopic and stillbirth care, not just loss as a reason to fear a later birth. Include repeated first trimesters, persistent pregnancy symptoms after loss, medical versus surgical management, failed treatment and administrative waiting. Do not impose a grief script: some name a baby, others name a traumatic medical process.

> Looking for some advice and stories from others. I am 28 years old and I have an almost 2 year old. I’m two weeks I will have to have a unilateral salpingectomy to remove my left fallopian tube due to hydrosalpinx. I had a miscarriage in December and the doctors think there was a hidden twin that was ectopic. My tube did not burst but I had internal bleeding and an infection. I thought it was just a bad miscarriage so I didn’t go to the ER for about a week and by then I was almost septic. My husband and I want more kids. Has anyone been able to have kids without IVF after having one tube removed? What happens to the eggs in that ovary? Do I need to freeze them or can they travel without the tube? I’m terrified that this means the end of growing my family. My doctor has reassured me that this will make it easier to get and stay pregnant (than it would be if I  kept the damaged tube in) but I wanted to hear from other women who have lived this and experienced this. Also any recovery advice would be appreciated. I have a baby shower to go to two days after my surgery and it’s a two hour drive. I really want to go but have no idea what to expect.
<!-- /verbatim -->

> Last week I went to my first OB appointment due to HG and just being all around miserable with the constant vomiting, well at the appointment the doctor said to do an ultrasound since we were already there she started with an couldn’t see anything so went transvaginal and pointed out the gestational sac as well as the yolk sac and that was it. She immediately went silent took a few more measurements and immediately said “I’m sorry”. She told me she thinks this is a blighted ovum based on my LMP (for them I should of been 12 weeks but measured 6+3) but since having my first born everything has been all over the place, the doctor told me that at least I have my first born 1F and honestly I’m so upset about it because I wanted this pregnancy so much, I go back tomorrow for a repeat scan to verify if it’s a missed miscarriage or not and I’m so scared part of me hopes they are so wrong just due to me not getting a very faint positive till a few weeks ago but the rational part of my brain is preparing for a loss. I don’t really know how to cope with the possible loss as me and my husband were so excited for this pregnancy. I am honestly just so upset because having HG would be for nothing I don’t get to see the end of the line with a new baby it just feels like this is all for nothing, I’ve been going through bouts of tears and anger, I just need help learning how to navigate life after loss I’m just thankful that my daughter is too young to understand what’s happening but it doesn’t make it hurt any less.

> I was terrified of surgery at first because it sounded much more extreme than taking medication. But once the D&C was properly explained to me, it actually felt like the less overwhelming option for me personally. The procedure itself took only a few minutes. I had it yesterday, bled a little afterwards, and today the bleeding has already stopped. I only have very mild cramps, haven't needed any pain medication and have been able to go about my normal day. Emotionally I also feel relieved. Of course I'm heartbroken and sad because this was a wanted pregnancy, but carrying a pregnancy that I knew was no longer developing was incredibly difficult for me. Having the procedure done has given me a sense of closure and feels like the beginning of healing.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu35-0374"></a>

<!-- verbatim:pbu35-0374 -->
> I want to explain something about how I’m processing all of this, because I sometimes feel as though my experience doesn’t quite fit the way miscarriage is usually talked about. I don’t feel ashamed to say that the experience has been traumatic, but I don’t feel as though I’m grieving the loss of a baby in the way some people describe. It was so early that I never saw anything on a scan beyond the gestational sac. I imagine that seeing a heartbeat or more of the pregnancy developing might have made it feel more real to me. It feels as though I’d only just processed the shock of finding out I was pregnant when we were told it probably wasn’t going to continue. There was barely any time to get my head around the idea of being pregnant before I was dealing with the uncertainty and reality of losing the pregnancy. 
> 
> I’m at peace with the fact that this pregnancy wasn’t meant to continue, but that doesn’t mean the experience itself hasn’t been difficult.

### 2. After loss, the milestones that matter are survival, location, a previous loss date and getting a baby home.

**Belongs in:** §§2, 6, 9; a loss-history branch within personalisation.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Trimester, pregnancy number and a calendar are not sufficient personalisation by themselves.”

**What should change or be retained:** Separate pregnancy count, living children, losses and their circumstances. A living child does not erase a loss; a previous induction for stillbirth is not experience of delivering a living baby. Preserve the different meanings of symptoms, movement, anniversaries and viability.

I’m just over 6 weeks now and had my first ultrasound today. Everything is tracking well and we saw the heartbeat. My husband and I nearly cried from relief and joy 🥹

I feel like this is a good sign that I’m healing and putting that episode behind me. I hope this gives hope to others that have had pregnancy scares, it really does get better! 🩵🩵

[pbu35-0178] [pbu35-0200] [pbu35-0328] [pbu35-0344]

### 3. Tests, scans, symptoms, apps and searches can briefly reassure, multiply uncertainty or become the next thing to check.

**Belongs in:** §§4, 6; reassurance, stories, search and AI.

**Relation:** confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “More information can reassure or renew fear.”

**What should change or be retained:** Specify the mechanisms: test-line comparison, LMP versus ovulation dates, non-diagnostic boutique imaging, optional measurements, heart-rate wearables, movement checking, out-of-hours results and algorithms. Purchased reassurance can create a diagnostic wait or reset one. Keep the cases where a scan helps and where people are satisfied with little testing.

They've mostly ruled out ectopic as they scanned my ovaries and couldn't see any abnormalities.
<!-- /verbatim -->

My average cycle length is 35 days, my LMP was 5th March and I had a positive ovulation test on 22nd March so, I am assuming I ovulated a day or 2 after this. <a id="pbu28-0109"></a>

> I had a private ultrasound on Monday and I had a 9mm gestational sac, are yolk sack present, but no fetal pole present.  
> I have also had bleeding which has been bright red and also changed to brown when wiping. It hasn’t been a lot of blood but enough to show concern.  
> I have visited the early pregnancy assessment unit today for another scan and they found the gestational sack and yolk sack and measured my gestational sack at 9.3mm which shows that the yolk sack has only grown 0.3mm in five days  
> I really do not have much hope for this pregnancy at this stage. I’m currently six weeks two days pregnant.   
> I have another private scan in a week time and another scan on the NHS in two weeks.  
> Considering the bleed and slow growth of the gestational sac, I am really worried and think this is going to result in a miscarriage.

<a id="pbu28-0220"></a>

> I need some words of encouragement or guidance if anyone has been through similar, went to my Dating scan today my last menstrual cycle was March 26. I’ve had progression in my hCG blood tests, but they were pretty low at first, but they were doubling. I thought I would be based off my menstrual cycle six weeks now but she said the baby is measuring at five weeks three days . I’ve been having cramps like mild period cramps that I told her about and for some reason she mentioned that it’s not by my C-section scar but higher up in my uterus (I googled that that’s actually a good thing)she said it might not be anything but she also mentioned that maybe it’s an early miscarriage she didn’t really specify why she would think so - so it has me confused. She wants me to come back in two weeks to do another ultrasound but that seems far away. Should I ask to do one sooner or to do blood draws to watch my HCG? When she pulled the wand out there was some spotting on there, but she said that happens sometimes from irritating the cervix - I have not had any spotting before. Now when I just went pee after getting home there was still some spotting. Has anyone had similar experience and it worked out to be OK? I’m laying in bed trying to not be emotional and depressed. I think also not having much nausea this pregnancy also had me worried as I’ve only really struggled with insane fatigue and hunger with sore breast and quick loss of breath when moving. I’m also 37 years old so I am considered geriatric pregnancy and I have two uteruses so I’m higher risk.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0287"></a>

I know rationally that it's still too early to be able to count on a certain movement pattern, but it's impossible not to once you start feeling movements. Today I was at work and hadn't felt much for maybe 2 hours and felt myself starting to panic more and more, so I lied about feeling sick and that I therefore needed to go home. Of course I googled and found a lot of horrible stories about umbilical cords that were around the neck or placentas that suddenly stopped working or came loose and about mothers who went in and found out that their baby had suddenly died.

> hi guys… just some background info i (20 F) took the first abortion pill mifeprestone on september 19th. i did not follow up with misoprostol, in fact i could not help hut feel so guilty that i reached out to get progesterone. i am currently on a progesterone dose of 200 mg taking 2 by mouth, and after the mifeprestone i had an ultrasound and heart beat and baby was developing normally. i am however EXTREMELY scared of a miscarriage. i know my chances are now higher and i so wish they werent. anyways, my typical symptoms include nausea, breast tenderness, sensitive smell, and slight food aversion. last night, i had wiped tp and there was a little spotting. like so little i had to use a flashlight to verify it was light pink and not discharge, but it was spotting. ive also experienced cramping. not heavy, just dull and its not constant. nothing like period cramps. im unsure if pregnancy symptoms going away are normal like this, and im just scared. im going to brush my teeth because that normally triggers instant throwing up, but yesterday morning i actually did not throw up. maybe im just anxiety ridden and this is my first pregnancy, but any advice would be extremely helpful & i would be so grateful. thank you!
<!-- /verbatim -->

### 4. Parents ask for different things: positive stories, honest good and bad outcomes, practical solutions, or space to vent.

**Belongs in:** §§4, 6, 10–11, 16.4, 17.6.

**Relation:** extends and qualifies.

**Current conclusion:** “They trust people and stories more than documents.”

**What should change or be retained:** Replace a simple source-format hierarchy with the job the information must do. Clinical explanations and lived experience can complement each other. Do not infer a demand for a guide from every reassurance question; do not make either positivity or worst-case detail the universal tone.

> I’m aware every pregnancy and every person is different but I’m still trying to gather some anecdotal evidence or stories from people who had cryptic or very easy pregnancies, and from people who are very athletic and in training (even if you are very athletic and pregnancy was not easy).. 
> 
> The comment I read today was from someone who was already eating protein based meals every hour or so, performing extreme mountain hikes regularly, and actively in training and didn’t know they were pregnant until month 7. my memory might be off by a month here.
> 
> Of course I’ve also read people who self identify as very unfit who had cryptic pregnancies and knew one woman myself who found out she was pregnant during labor (what a wild email to open that day!). 
> 
> What was your pregnancy experience like? What was your exercise level like? Your diet? How far along were you? Did you notice differences in first vs other trimesters (obviously not super relevant to cryptic pregnancies but still)

<a id="pbu28-0075"></a>

**[pbu35-0314]**

> Part of me thinks that 19 is incredibly young and that having a baby now would completely change the life I imagined for the next few years. I know that being financially capable of raising a child doesn’t automatically mean I’m ready to become a mother.

> I’m not asking Reddit to make the decision for me, but I would really appreciate perspectives from people who have been in a similar situation, especially people who became parents young or who had to choose between continuing or terminating an unexpected pregnancy.

**Further supporting IDs** (including records quoted under another pattern):

[pbu17-0038] [pbu17-0098] [pbu17-0197] [pbu17-0198] [pbu17-0208] [pbu17-0214] [pbu17-0257] [pbu17-0271] [pbu17-0284] [pbu17-0351] [pbu22-0245] [pbu22-0284]

[pbu22-0303] [pbu28-0284] [pbu28-0409] [pbu28-0443] [pbu35-0070] [pbu35-0208] [pbu35-0236] [pbu35-0307] [pbu35-0308]

### 5. Wanting a child, planning conception and wanting to continue this pregnancy now are separate questions.

**Belongs in:** §§1, 2, 6, 9–10; add reproductive decisions and adoption.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “She is pregnant, often for the first time”

**What should change or be retained:** Start the reader account before an assumed commitment to continuation or parenting. Planned conception, infertility, IVF and prior loss can coexist with dread, regret, wishing for miscarriage, abortion or adoption. A supportive partner and adequate finances do not settle readiness. Keep relief without regret alongside grief and conflicted decisions.

**Verbatim evidence:**

<a id="pbu17-0031"></a>
**[pbu17-0031]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu17-0031 -->
> Six years ago, I had an abortion very early in pregnancy. I have NEVER felt bad about my decision. At that time, I didn’t have financial stability, my mental health was not in the best place, I had no support system, no health insurance, and I didn’t even have $100 in my bank account.
> 
> In my opinion, bringing a child into that situation would not have been a viable choice.
> 
> Now I am married and expecting a baby with my husband. My circumstances and my maturity are completely different. This time, my motherhood is wanted and something I feel ready for.
> 
> The reason I’m posting this is because I don’t know if it’s hormones or something else, but seeing some videos where womens saying they want to “take back their vote,” or calling other women murderers for making that choice has been affecting me more than I expected.
> 
> Some of those messages honestly feel like they can mess with your head psychologically, even when you feel at peace with your own decision.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu17-0054"></a>
**[pbu17-0054]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu17-0054 -->

I know he’d prefer if I had the baby and was excited when we found out. Despite this, especially after seeing the toll this has taken on my mental health, he is backing me 100% whatever I choose.

On one hand we would have a baby by Christmas, but the glimpse of possible relief and returning to my ‘old life’ is so tempting; however, would we even return to our ‘normal’ or will this be a stain on our seemingly wonderful relationship?

> Please no judgement. I know I am young, but please no judgement. I am 21(f) and I am pregnant with our 3rd baby. I had our 1st at 19, second at 20, this one at 21. I got married at 18 when my husband was in the military. I love my children to pieces, I couldn’t imagine life without them and I prayed for them. Ever since getting pregnant, and starting my journey through getting my bachelors and eventually law school, I find myself…. regretful?? This pregnancy is already wrecking me mentally and abortion is not an option for us. I just am not sure what to do now. I am a stay at home mom for now and will be for the next 6 years till i’m done with schooling. I am just so stressed and sad and angry all the time about having yet another baby. I was so excited to be able to have my time at night, a glass of wine and a book. or finally not be pregnant for a summer. now I am 12 weeks pregnant and so upset about it. Sometimes I am excited, but other times I am so mad this happened. We tried for our first two, I had a miscarriage and d&c while trying to conceive our first, then another miscarriage before our second. I genuinely wanted my baby’s, now I sometimes wish I never had them and it makes me so sad that those thoughts even cross my mind.
<!-- /verbatim -->

But I really don't know how I will balance my job and my child in the future. Even stay-at-home moms struggle a lot, and financially I have no chance of quitting my job. I couldn't sleep in the middle of the night, I panicked right now. I dont k ow what to do

**[pbu28-0126]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0126 -->

I have to get labs drawn again tomorrow and another ultrasound on Friday. Trying to take things 1 minute at a time. Longer than that seems too overwhelming.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0169"></a>
**[pbu28-0169]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0169 -->
> I held off a getting an abortion because I knew my partner wanted to keep the baby. I never ended up getting one but now that it’s too late I find myself feeling very stressed, panicked, and upset most days. I am 21 and just don’t feel like I’m ready to have a baby, but I also felt guilty about wanting an abortion so I never got one. I am 26/27 weeks now. I don’t know how to get past these feelings. I am not generally a nervous person and this is the first time I’ve felt genuinely panicked and upset over something. I feel like I didn’t make the right decision for myself but there is no going back. I am still in school and have some time left before I finish my degree. My relationship with my partner is not 100%. I don’t have a great support system as all of my family lives abroad and I am on my own, aside from my partner and his family. My partner keeps telling me it is what it is and I just need to accept things and they’ll be okay but I genuinely feel horrible and like my world is ending. I don’t know what to do now. I feel like every day I have moments where I just feel so upset and panicked and it’s been getting harder to focus on things like school and getting out of the house.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0213"></a>
**[pbu28-0213]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0213 -->
> Has anyone here had a failed abortion? I took mifepristone and misoprostol pills at 9 weeks pregnant. I experienced significant cramping and pain after the first dose of misoprostol but only passed one medium clot and had minimal bleeding afterwards. My pregnancy tests are still positive. I am going to get an ultrasound to see if it was successful. I know abortions rarely fail, but sometimes they do. I will continue the pregnancy if my pill failed. I already felt immense regret, guilt, and sadness after making the decision I did. Part of me is holding onto hope for this baby
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0237"></a>
**[pbu28-0237]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0237 -->
> I'm currently between 6 and 7 weeks, doctor is not totally sure. When I found out, my first thought was that my life is over. My breasts have been sensitive for a month now, I am in bad mood all the time, I'm either angry, grumpy, depressed or so anxious that I have to really work for it to not become a panic attack. And every one of my friends I told so far, were so damn happy and crying from joy, because I am pregnant, but I hate it, I feel so not seen and not heard... tell me I'm not alone
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0238"></a>
**[pbu28-0238]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0238 -->
> I know, but it is far more complicated than that. And believe me, I was considering it. I want to have kids some day, but preferably not right now. My husband really wants a kid right now, as he is a decade older and is worried that he might not have energy to be a hands on father later, which I can understand. Basically, it boils down to, do I want to not have this kid so much, that I would be okay with getting divorced? And honestly, the answer is no. Even if I hate it, even if I feel like total shit, I would still rather suffer through this, than leave my husband, who is a good husband and this is one of his very few hard boundaries...?
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0269"></a>
**[pbu28-0269]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0269 -->

> Because I would love to have this child but I’m young and I need to think rationally
<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu28-0295]**

I want to go back school but the program is 6 months and I won’t be able to enroll til January. I would be due in March/ April. Is it possible to be in school while having a newborn?
<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu28-0329]**

However I am full of regret. I just don’t know what to do. Has anyone been in this situation
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0426"></a>
**[pbu28-0426]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0426 -->
> Some context, I’ve never been the type to FEEL like I was put here to be a mom. This is the first pregnancy that Ive decided to continue and I’m 11 weeks pregnant and the first trimester has whooped my ass to shreds. Absolutely miserable for months. I’ve also been spotting and cramping every other week, each ultrasound shows nothing and baby is always fine. But I’m not nervous of miscarrying, honestly, I don’t think I would care if I did. I tell my OB bc my husband wants me to. My husband, extremely supportive partner, is worried that I don’t feel a connection. I feel like I’m being punished somehow for not yearning to be a mother or have a child. Maybe bc I’ve had a such a rough start, that I don’t feel any joy. I thought after finding out we’re having a girl would make me feel something but instead I just feel the nausea, the fatigue, constipation, and dread going to work. I’m not depressed, I know this bc I’ve had a long history with my mental health, I just don’t feel for this child like I think I should. And I know pregnancy and parenthood are two different things, so I’m hoping as we move along and I meet my baby, I’ll be over joyed.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0427"></a>
**[pbu28-0427]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0427 -->

I’m scared because I am not excited at all I am just terrified and actually feeling extremely depressed and anxious. It’s still early about 7 weeks so anything can happen but the feelings I am going through now are just bad, am I still kind of postpartum? What’s wrong with me, I’ve never had such weird feelings of deep sadness.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0429"></a>
**[pbu28-0429]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0429 -->
> I think that’s part of why I’m so scared. Once a baby is here, I can’t just change my mind and go back to my old life. I wish I could see into the future and know whether everything will work out, Whether I’ll be happy, Whether I’ll be a good mom, And whether I’ll regret my decision. I don’t want to make a life changing decision purely out of fear but I also don’t want to ignore my fears just because I initially wanted this pregnancy.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu35-0247"></a>
**[pbu35-0247]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu35-0247 -->
> I just found out that I’m pregnant today. The thing is, is that I’m eighteen and have illnesses that would put my life in jeopardy if I were to go through with having this kid. 
> 
> I just went to the hospital today for extreme abdominal pains and I was on the floor in the emergency room sobbing with my boyfriend because I didn’t know why I hurt so bad. 
> 
> The fact that I’m so far along and didn’t have any symptoms besides the extreme pain today says a lot about how the pregnancy would go. My boyfriend is so supportive and he’s paying for the entire thing which I’m so grateful for. 
> 
> I can’t tell my family because they have strong views and I don’t feel like I would get any support from them. I just really wanted to share with anyone who might listen or went through the same thing possibly?
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu35-0277"></a>
**[pbu35-0277]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu35-0277 -->
> When I found out I was pregnant I was super scared but I wanted to continue the pregnancy. My partner did not and has continued talking me out of it since the moment we found out. He says things like “I’m not ready” “we’re not financially stable” “you don’t seem to understand the reality of having a child” “our relationship isn’t mature enough”. He also told his mom about the pregnancy and she contributed a lot to his negative outlook on having a child.  
> He is very sad about me getting an abortion and he cries and gets emotional but I don’t think his emotions come close to what I’m feeling.   
> I wanted to keep the baby but I refuse to bring it into a world where one parent doesn’t want it and the other is fighting to make ends meet, it’s just not fair.   
> But it’s so hard for me because I can feel my body changing and I have all of these pregnancy symptoms and it feels like it’s all for nothing. My heart hurts so bad.   
> I get why he feels the way he does but it breaks my heart that he won’t even entertain the thought of keeping the child for one second.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu35-0280"></a>
**[pbu35-0280]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu35-0280 -->

<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu35-0313]**

>

He is my best friend. I worry that keeping something this significant from him could eventually eat at me, and if he ever found out accidentally, he might feel deeply betrayed and never forgive me for it.

[pbu35-0366]

### 6. Some parents face threats, sexual coercion, violence or control over whether pregnancy continues and whether they can get care.

**Belongs in:** §§3, 5–6, 9–10, 12; add coercion and safety as their own subsection.

**Relation:** contradicts the sufficiency of the task-list interpretation; extends the restored caveat.

**Current conclusion:** “From her side, the gap is mental load more than absence”

**What should change or be retained:** Retain ordinary mental-load accounts but stop using them as the umbrella for all partner problems. Pressure runs toward abortion and toward continuation, and can come from relatives or clinicians. Physical obstruction, withholding money, threatened custody or suicide and violence are not failures of reading format or household coordination.

**[pbu22-0046]**

**[pbu22-0047]**

<a id="pbu22-0140"></a>
**[pbu22-0140]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0140 -->
> Everyone told me to get an abortion. My family said they’d never forgive me if I didn’t. People said she wouldn’t be healthy and that I was selfish.
> 
> I made an appointment. I couldn’t take the pills. I was almost relieved when I had to schedule a D&C weeks out. I spent those weeks trying to convince myself it was the right thing—that she gave me a second chance and I should let her go.
> 
> But I couldn’t.
> 
> I blocked everyone out and chose to keep her.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0155"></a>
**[pbu22-0155]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0155 -->
> This is just to vent because I genuinely feel like I’m gonna lose it. I’m 7 months pregnant a sahm to a three year old and in a very toxic relationship. He is very verbal abusive, financial abusive. I don’t have anyone to talk to. I left my job because he said he would provide and take care of everything and now everyday I’m being told I don’t do anything, I’m a fat cunt, the list goes on and on. He hasn’t bought anything for this new baby, says that I can buy it when I go back to work. He says two weeks after I give birth is a good time to go back. Feeling very stuck and I hate this.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0195"></a>
**[pbu22-0195]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0195 -->
> I’m around 10 weeks pregnant and I genuinely don’t know what the fuck to do. I haven’t even confronted him yet because he has no idea that I know. I’ve just been laying here next to him feeling sick, staring at him, wondering who the hell I’ve been sleeping beside all this time.
> 
> I feel disgusting. I feel stupid. I feel humiliated. I feel like I wasn’t enough. I keep asking myself how someone can come home, tell you they love you, talk about your baby, hold you while you’re throwing up from pregnancy, and then turn around and do this behind your back.
> 
> And the fucked up part is that I still love him, which makes me hate myself even more.
> 
> I don’t know if I want to stay. I don’t know if I want to leave. I don’t even know if I want this pregnancy anymore, and saying that out loud makes me feel like the worst person in the world.
> 
> I just feel trapped and completely broken. I don’t know who to talk to, and I don’t know how I’m supposed to act normal when my entire world just exploded.
> 
> Has anyone else gone through something like this? How did you survive it?
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0236"></a>
**[pbu22-0236]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0236 -->
> My daughter (16F) has become pregnant. From what we’ve been able to piece together, it happened at a party. Unfortunately, date-rape drugs are fairly common in Italy. We obviously filed a police report against unknown persons, but we’ll likely never know who the piece of shit is that got her into this mess. She chose to tell only me (I’m her father), and as of now (7 weeks in), her mother still doesn’t know about the pregnancy. 
> 
> Abortion is legal here in Italy, though the issue of parental consent is a bit of a grey area. We tried to get permission going through a whole series of steps that would be a waste of time to describe and I thought everything was going to be fine. But it wasn't. My daughter practically ran out of the clinic in tears, and now she says she’s "not sure about what she’s doing." 
> 
> We have to hurry, we’re running out of time. And I’m terrified that she might keep the baby. It’s not about a lack of money. It just seems completely absurd to me. In a few days, we’ll be forced to tell her mother, who will rightly be furious with me. What a disaster.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0299"></a>
**[pbu22-0299]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0299 -->

We were never in a serious official relationship, more like talking/casual for over a year now. When I found out I was pregnant, abortion never crossed my mind. I planned to keep it but now hearing his side, idk what to do. He told me to tell him my decision by Sunday night. I feel like abortion would be a traumatizing event that I’ll regret and feel guilty about for the rest of my life
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0300"></a>
**[pbu22-0300]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0300 -->

<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0304"></a>
**[pbu22-0304]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0304 -->
> Recently found out I am unexpectedly pregnant (31) and my boyfriend (30) has completely lost it. Since finding out 2 weeks ago, he has not been supportive and has insisted the only path forward is abortion. He has been manipulative the entire time, making a lot of threats around his mental health and I haven't had a voice to share my thoughts or feelings. He says I am not looking out for his best interests and not choosing him when it comes to proceeding with the pregnancy. Apparently his rationale is just that the timing isn't right and he's not ready. Well is anyone ever truly ready? I feel as though we have good careers and were in what I thought was a good relationship, so we would have a good foundation to have a child. There is no talking to him, or having him see another perspective. I have reached out to my support network and have been seeing a counsellor too.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0306"></a>
**[pbu22-0306]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0306 -->
> When I told my BD, He was extremely upset. He actually gave me a very large amount of money to get an abortion, Which was honestly so strange because we were TRYING for a baby. We used ovulation strips. I took prenatals. We weren’t actively trying at the exact moment I got pregnant anymore but we also weren’t preventing it. He had previously told me he wanted a child, We talked about baby things, All that stuff. A couple of days before I found out I was pregnant, We got into a disagreement. I had been extremely emotional and I finally opened up to him about some things I had been struggling with. I normally don’t open up to him much because I feel like he can be judgmental and doesn’t always think before he speaks. The next day, He told me that I’m “always negative” and was upset that I wasn’t responding to his good morning snaps.  
> Like…what? I was hurt and I definitely said things during that argument that I didn’t necessarily mean but some of the things he said to me were genuinely hurtful especially considering I had just been vulnerable with him. He now says that argument is the reason he changed his mind about having a baby but that’s not what it is and I know it. Since finding out I’m pregnant, His emotions surrounding it have been extremely immature. He’s said things like, “Well no one really wants single moms” He’s told me I’m “miserable” He’s criticized my baby names and said some of them aren’t “real names” However he has agreed that he will be intimately involved in our child’s life and I don’t doubt this since he’s the kind of person who couldn’t live with themselves if they were a deadbeat. There are definitely moments where I get scared and think, “What if I’m making the wrong decision?” “What if I should just get an abortion?” But I’m not having an abortion. I’ve made my decision.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0315"></a>
**[pbu22-0315]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0315 -->

>

<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0080"></a>
**[pbu28-0080]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0080 -->
> I’ve wanted a second child for so long now. My body not allowing me to get pregnant is almost like the universe screaming GET THE HELL OUT! WHAT ARE YOU THINKING? I’ve been pretty unfulfilled and unhappy for the last 3 years or so. I’m completely checked out. Every time I try to spin things in a positive way my husband says something so stupid or does something awful and I’m like.. what the fuck are you still doing in this situation.. he isn’t abusive, not a bad guy at all. But he’s really just emotionally drained me as well as a lot of external factors like his life before me (ex wife, kids, etc.) he’s also really made me unsafe in my own home by pushing my boundaries (which I’ve posted about on here before) we have sex when I ovulate, that’s it. I don’t enjoy it. I don’t even change in front of him because his lack of respect with my body boundaries in the past.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0173"></a>
**[pbu28-0173]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0173 -->
> The headache continued into the next morning, and when I woke up, I could not stop throwing up from the pain. It was almost nonstop. I decided I needed to go back to the ER. My boyfriend told me I didn’t need to go, that people were in pain all the time and didn’t go in, and that if I ate I would feel better. I could not eat because I could not stop vomiting. He wouldn’t listen to me and told me he wouldn’t be driving me to the ER. I said okay and ordered a Lyft because I couldn’t drive. He then tried to physically body block me from leaving the apartment to go. I was in so much pain, sobbing, and he was just standing there acting like I was being crazy, not letting me leave.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0174"></a>
**[pbu28-0174]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0174 -->

He calls the doctors incompetent, that they would be better in his hometown, says something I’m doing must be causing it. He literally brought up me eating popcorn every night as a bedtime snack as an example of what could be causing it lol. He then says he’s going to talk to his friend on the phone and goes to the garage to drink beers and call his friend, leaving me alone again to cry about how alone I am.
<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu28-0175]**

I love him, but i do not see this as a super long term relationship. not sure what all that context really gives you, but any response is appreciated, as rude, kind, dissapointed, or supporting, assuming or not, as it may be.

<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0292"></a>
**[pbu28-0292]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0292 -->

<a id="pbu28-0338"></a>
**[pbu28-0338]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0338 -->
> He convinced me to quit my job so I could be a stay-at-home mom. Later I realized it wasn’t really about preparing for a family—it was because he didn’t want me working around other men. He became increasingly controlling. I lost friends, and since I’m an immigrant, my entire family lives on another continent. I became completely isolated.  
> Then I got pregnant.  
> Instead of becoming more loving, he became angrier. Our arguments turned into him becoming explosive. He would take my pillows and blankets so I couldn’t sleep, yell at me for hours, and make me feel trapped. Because I was so sleep deprived, I started napping during the day, and then he would criticize me by saying other pregnant women worked while I “slept all day.”
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0339"></a>
**[pbu28-0339]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0339 -->
> His mother also completely broke my trust. I thought she was a kind person, but she defended him after he left bruises on me while trying to force me out of the house at 4 a.m. while I was pregnant, with nowhere to go. To her, he just “grabbed me too hard.” To me, it was terrifying.  
> I never thought I’d admit this, but there were moments when I questioned whether I could continue this pregnancy—not because I don’t want my baby, but because I hated the idea of being tied to him forever. That thought alone fills me with guilt.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0341"></a>
**[pbu28-0341]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0341 -->
> We got home, things were kinda normal, but then out of the blue he texted me to say he didn’t want a child and wanted to break up with me if I had it. He said he wouldn’t ever pay me child support, and if I tried to take it through CMS (Child Maintenance Service in the UK), he would kill himself. I told him that I’d see what my options were for a termination, but I was sure I was at least 9 weeks at this point and didn’t want to go through surgery. I also didn’t want a termination at all.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0344"></a>
**[pbu28-0344]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0344 -->

I have been in an abusive situation where I have been assaulted regularly since February and the violence and ideation of murder have gotten worse.   We have been having sex on a regular basis each month and haven’t had any luck.    I am really hoping that it does not happen this month after we separated and divorce papers have been filed.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0428"></a>
**[pbu28-0428]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0428 -->
> My baby’s father and I mutually agreed to try for a baby. We actively tried for a while, And when it didn’t happen, We eventually stopped tracking ovulation and putting so much pressure on it. That’s when I got pregnant. I was excited. I thought this was something we both wanted because he was the one who suggested trying in the first place. However, When I told him I was pregnant, He wasn’t nearly as happy as I expected. He did a lot of things to convince me to have an abortion but I ultimately decided I wanted to continue the pregnancy. He’s since told me he wants a paternity test. I’m not upset about taking one because I know I haven’t been with anyone else so I have nothing to hide.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0433"></a>
**[pbu28-0433]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0433 -->

For weeks they pushed for me to terminate and it was so stressful, every conversation being “get rid of it.” I even actually went to an appointment bc I realized some of what they said was realistic and at the time I thought maybe it wasn’t a bad idea for us to figure our crap out more and become more stable.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0434"></a>
**[pbu28-0434]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0434 -->

The goal was for me to stay at my parents for a little longer and my partner to stay w them and start looking for a new job, setting up the room etc, and I would visit every time I had a chunk of days off from work.
<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu28-0439]**

<a id="pbu35-0018"></a>
**[pbu35-0018]**

<a id="pbu35-0019"></a>

<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu35-0138"></a>
**[pbu35-0138]**

<a id="pbu35-0172"></a>

<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu35-0173"></a>
**[pbu35-0173]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu35-0173 -->
> The only “help” I technically have here is him, and that’s not really help at all. I don’t trust him, and I don’t feel safe or supported relying on him for anything.
>

It feels like losing my independence and my peace, even if it comes with some stability.

>

> I feel like I’m constantly in survival mode and I don’t see a “good” option—just two really hard ones.

**[pbu35-0180]**

**[pbu35-0198]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu35-0198 -->
> Then she suddenly said something like, “There are many nights lately where I go to bed trying not to kill myself“ & basically tried to end the conversation there by leaving the phone call. (My sister still lives with her and told my my mother left the room)
> 
> I don’t want her to feel that way & I take statements like that seriously. But in that moment, it felt like the conversation immediately shifted from me expressing hurt to me suddenly being unable to say anything at all without looking cruel.
> 
> Afterward, I said to my siblings that it felt like a “shut up” argument. Like something said to immediately end accountability & make me back down emotionally.
> 
> At that point my sister suddenly started yelling at me, saying I was being unfair and insensitive because our mother struggles mentally and that I had no right to call it manipulative.
<!-- /verbatim -->

### 7. Being partnered or having leave does not mean practical help, emotional safety or recovery time is available.

**Belongs in:** §§5, 10 For 6, 16.1, 17.1.

**Relation:** confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Format helps the partner who wants to learn; it does not create the wish”

**What should change or be retained:** Add actual care tasks, recovery assumptions and withheld emotional support. Paid leave can be spent holding the baby and sleeping while the recovering parent does the work. A partner can attend every appointment or provide money and still leave betrayal or emotional pain unresolved.

[pbu35-0170] [pbu35-0215] [pbu35-0249] [pbu35-0358]

### 8. Partners can be active advocates and affected parents who need explanations, updates, recognition and support themselves.

**Belongs in:** §§5, 9; partner voice and contrary evidence.

**Relation:** confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The gap includes practical support during pain, illness and recovery and partners’ own fear and exhaustion.”

**What should change or be retained:** Include fathers' own grief, hidden panic, post-birth symptoms and treatment-seeking, not only instructions for assisting her. Keep supportive decision-making, shared care, memory-keeping and increased teamwork. Practical action and emotional presence are distinct jobs.

**Verbatim evidence:**

<a id="pbu17-0126"></a>
**[pbu17-0126]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu17-0126 -->
> Two weeks is barely enough time to understand what has happened, let alone recover from the birth, support your partner, learn how to care for a newborn and deal with complications when they happen. I'm incredibly grateful that I've been able to be here with my wife and son, but going back to normal life after two weeks feels incredibly difficult when your family is still finding its feet.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu17-0127"></a>
**[pbu17-0127]**

Needless to say that my mental state leading up to my baby’s December due date was the worst it’s ever been. I was very happy when my little one was born though.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0232"></a>
**[pbu22-0232]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0232 -->

I see so many posts of women saying they freaked out on their husbands their whole pregnancy and laughing about it like it was a big joke but I don’t find that funny. We’re supposed to be a team and I know I’m not the one going through the physical challenges but I’m working hard to provide for the family that we’re soon to have and working 60+ hours a week to make sure we’re set up for an easy transition into our new life while being constantly verbally berated when I don’t believe I’ve done anything wrong is taking a serious toll on me already.

But I can’t shake the anxiety - I’m having trouble eating and sleeping, am experiencing intense chest pain, and am in a state of panic most of the time. I feel like I am already failing in keeping my wife and daughter safe and secure even though I know logically that isn’t true or fair to myself. I have already reached out to a therapist and made a dr appointment to talk about anxiety meds. I guess I’m experiencing post partum anxiety?

[pbu35-0386]

### 9. Knowing to call or attend does not guarantee an appointment, admission, transport, childcare, affordable care or usable follow-up.

**Belongs in:** §§5, 8–10, 16.2, 17.2 and 17.4.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Interpretation: one page per country on who does what and when to call is content nobody in the competitor set sells.”

**What should change or be retained:** Extend contacts to actual access and contingency: referral prerequisites, unreturned calls, records, service closures, travel, unavailable childcare and inability to pay. A dated plan can be prepared correctly and still collapse when a hospital changes the date. Late discovery and emergency admission can remove the assumed months of preparation.

> Hi everyone! I am a social worker and former public defender and I’m interested in becoming a certified abortion doula. It’s something I’ve been thinking about for years, ever since I learned it was something that existed. I admire birth doulas and full-spectrum doulas hugely, but abortion work is something I’ve always wanted to get back into and this a way of doing it that really aligns with my values. I realize it will likely be mostly volunteer work unless I’m somehow able to form some kind of partnership with a clinic and I’m okay with that as I am currently employed. I have been doing a little bit of research and I came across Rouge Doulas, which was recommended to me as a starting point for training, as well as Allo Doula Academy, BirthPro Full Spectrum Training, and The Lemon Grove. Does anyone have experience with any of these or recommendations for any other training programs? Thanks in advance!
<!-- /verbatim -->

[pbu35-0377] [pbu35-0378]

### 10. Being listened to, receiving a reason and getting conflicting instructions resolved matter as much as receiving a result.

**Belongs in:** §§4–6, 10; being believed and care that explains nothing.

**Relation:** confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Care navigation includes failed referrals and follow-up, dismissive or blaming treatment, unexplained results and conflicting professionals.”

**What should change or be retained:** Add the effect on behaviour: self-doubt, shame about asking again, hiding symptoms to get booked, avoiding hospitals or stopping treatment. Record alleged error, diagnosis and medical advice as testimony, not clinical adjudication. Keep clinicians who explain, investigate and give direct contact as counter-evidence.

I’m starting to get really sick of this cause everything that they state that insurance should cover, my insurance has not been covering and it’s costing me an insane amount of money. 
> What do I do?  I’ve already requested to transfer to a different hospital and they told me absolutely not. I’m at a loss and feel constantly guilty.
<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu28-0221]**

Im waiting for my OB to call back, which can sometimes take hours depending on how busy they are. I just wanna get started on treatment. Has anyone dealt with this before?

**[pbu28-0222]**

I was a little bit taken aback because had I not had those draws done, she would've convinced me something was wrong by her initial statement. Now she was telling me to stop getting them all together.

<a id="pbu28-0223"></a>
**[pbu28-0223]**

<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu28-0236]**

<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0366"></a>
**[pbu28-0366]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0366 -->

I was really hoping for a callback today though since I’m looking for some answers. I’m scheduled to come in for a prenatal visit in 2 weeks, but I can’t wait that long!!
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0384"></a>
**[pbu28-0384]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0384 -->
> So now for the literal nightmare: So at my very first OB appointment I get labs done. Now I specifically asked to have an iron study done as I’ve historically have struggled with anemia. Ferritin comes back at a 4.6. Midwife tells me to just take iron supplements. Also to note at my first appointment I told the Midwife that I have hyper mobile ehlers danlos syndrome (hEDS). It’s never affected me too badly just little things like shoulders and knees occasionally dislocating. When I told her she said “what’s that?” And I explained it was a connective tissue disorder and she said “oh it’s not important”. (It was in fact very important)
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0385"></a>
**[pbu28-0385]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0385 -->
> Now my next appointment I missed which was my fault, I was just so sick I could not get myself to go. After I missed it I called my clinic explaining and they’re like okay next available appointment is 4 weeks out. So in my head I’m like oh so I’m just missing a month of prenatal care? But that was the earliest they had so I guess I just had to deal with it. The next appointment comes around and a few hours before I get a phone call from the clinic saying, “oh sorry the Midwife just isn’t coming in so we have to cancel your appointment, the next available is 3 weeks out”. Now I’m annoyed and pissed but honestly at the time I was so focused on work I was just like whatever okay fine. NEXT APPOINTMENT comes around and at this point I am 23 weeks pregnant coming in for my anatomy scan. When I check in I’m told that the Midwife isn’t here today and we’re gonna have to reschedule. So I get my scan but my husband and I weren’t told if anything was okay or any information about the baby. The get me an appointment for the next week.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0386"></a>
**[pbu28-0386]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0386 -->
> One night at work when I was 26 weeks, I was sitting at my desk and my heart rate randomly went from 65 to 230. Thankfully as someone who used to be an emt I was able to do a valsalva maneuver on myself and get my heart rate down to the 90s. Ambulance gets there and takes me to the hospital and of course they send me to OB triage and not the ER where I have another episode but they don’t have me hooked up to a cardiac monitor. Eventually in the ER the doc tells me that “pregnancy isn’t in his wheelhouse” and can’t help me, but he gives me a referral for cardiology and checked my ferritin which is now a 3. I was hardcore failing orthostatic vitals, just trying to sit up in the bed my heart rate would skyrocket to 140, but they get me in a wheelchair and send me home.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0387"></a>
**[pbu28-0387]**

I have appointments every week: cardiologist, electrophysiologist, mfm, high risk OB, and my pcp. The plan now is to try and get me as strong as possible before I give birth, induce me as there are concerns with me going into natural labor while being on an anti arrhythmic medication, and keep me in the hospital for at least a month until I get a cardiac ablation surgery after I I give birth.
<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu28-0398]**

I just tried to breathe, but was concerned I might need an ambulance, especially if I passed out or spiked a fever. Thankfully I did not. My husband called the OB, not helpful. Just sent a list of acceptable medications for constipation.
<!-- /verbatim -->

> 
> Is it standard to look at it during the 20-week anatomy scan?
> 
> Should I proactively push my OB/Midwife to check it sooner, given my history of early losses?
> 
> I have an appointment coming up with my midwife and plan to bring this up, but I would love to hear your experiences. Did anyone manage to get early or extra checks just for peace of mind after IVF/early loss? Thank you

<a id="pbu35-0302"></a>
**[pbu35-0302]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu35-0302 -->
> I finally wrote one last plea to my doctor - I can’t work I feel So sick, this can’t be normal. They got me in for an ultrasound that day.
> 
> And there was the sack still hanging out- now with a fetal pole.  No heartbeat, which i thought should absolutely be there by 7 weeks, but they said I’m measuring a week behind and they’ll check next week.
> 
> At least when i thought i miscarried I had closure.  Now I am back to waiting.  I have very little hope of this being a successful pregnancy considering I’m measuring behind.  I have no joy.
> 
> I’m also angry at the nurses and doctor who just kept telling me that I felt sick because my hormones were regulating- no one thought to suggest maybe I did not in fact miscarry until I begged them to check.  My therapist even suggested I look into a temporary prescription for Xanax.
> 
> I’m just sharing to vent.  Maybe also to encourage women to advocate for themselves.  Pregnancy is so out of our control it’s frustrating.  Now I go on feeling awful just to wait for the next ultrasound.
<!-- /verbatim -->

[pbu35-0300] [pbu35-0349] [pbu35-0350] [pbu35-0387] [pbu35-0390]

### 11. Prenatal distress can include suicidal thoughts, self-harm, rage and inability to function during a wanted pregnancy.

**Belongs in:** §§2, 6, 9–10; add explicit prenatal mental-health severity and disclosure barriers.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The restored words include panic, sadness, lack of excitement, body distress, regret and fear of not bonding or surviving birth.”

**What should change or be retained:** Do not reduce these accounts to ordinary mood changes or make postpartum the first mental-health checkpoint. Include concealed self-harm, death wishes, loss of established coping strategies, effects on other children and the parent's uncertainty about depression versus pregnancy symptoms. Do not infer that ambivalence is always illness, or diagnose from a post.

I’m so fucking afraid I have caused damage and there’s no way to safely make it to term. I feel like I’m mourning. I feel like I’ve failed this baby entirely.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0242"></a>
**[pbu28-0242]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0242 -->
> I don’t know if I truly know how to explain what’s going on, but I’m going to try my best. Starting in the third trimester, I began getting these fleeting feelings of panic. Like, “this feels wrong, I gotta get out of here, get this baby out of me, I need to run away from everything” panic. It’s like a pit in my stomach and intense anxiety. I love my baby, my life, my husband. Everything is perfect and I’m so excited to welcome my little girl into the world. I don’t understand these random moments. I feel intense guilt after, like I’m already a bad mother or a terrible person. I don’t know if this is relatable, but I just wanted to see if anyone else has felt something similar. I guess I just feel so ashamed and wanted to see if this is a me thing.
<!-- /verbatim -->

It started becoming worse when I realized I have no way of coping with it. I used to drink or smoke weed to numb the sad feeling and I’ve stopped completely after finding out I’m pregnant. I feel so stuck in a situation where my body isn’t mine at the same time feeling ungrateful for a life I’ve created. All these thoughts have been keeping me up everyday as well as the backaches. I’ve been sleeping for about 3-4 hours a day, usually broken up into 1 hour sessions.

### 12. Medication choices are trade-offs between feared fetal harm, side effects, daily functioning and fear of being judged unfit.

**Belongs in:** §§2, 4–6, 8–10; treatment and disclosure.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The broken week-by-week script includes persistent pain, exhaustion, vomiting and incontinence, repeated remedies and disrupted sleep.”

**What should change or be retained:** Include stopping established treatment, failed remedies, adverse effects attributed by an author, changing treatment and supportive prescribing. The parent may trust the prescriber and still want peer experience. Fear of CPS, lost care or involuntary admission can block disclosure; these feared consequences are not established legal or medical facts.

**Verbatim evidence:**

> We had a hell of a day Monday, anything that could go wrong was going wrong. By naptime I was spent, but my son would not go to sleep. I had intrusive thoughts of wanting to smack him across the face and hurt him. I didn’t. Immediately I told him I loved him and would be back in a couple of minutes. I left him in his room and called my wife hysterical because of that thought. 
> 
> My midwife said it’s probably time to think about medication if I’m comfortable. I agreed that it’s time because the herbal remedies are clearly not working as well. Here’s where I am asking for advice? I’ve tried two SSRIs in the past and they did not make me feel better. 
> 
> Secondary midwife suggested trying Cymbalta. She’s got me on the lowest possible dose, went over possible side-effects, and says the benefit at this point outweighs the risks. I took my first dose tonight, but I am scared of what this is going to do to my baby and to me after delivery. I’ve read about possibility of more bleeding, baby having withdraw symptoms, difficulty breathing… it’s a lot. I believe in science and medicine, and would be far less freaked out if it were just me. 
> 
> Has anyone here been on Cymbalta during pregnancy or PP? What was your experience? Did you breastfeed while on it? Any experiences would be appreciated. I’m just trying to ease my mind that everything will more than likely be okay, while trying to not beat myself up over Monday.

> For a few days I was taking Metoclopramide and Zofran but was still unwell. Meto gave me restless legs so I need to stop it. I went to my pharmacist and they gave me B6 and unisom - I took 1/2 a tablet which worked for 2 days I literally went and ate so much in those 2 days. Day 3 I woke up throwing up so increased to 1 whole tablet which again worked wonders but then I started to develop the weird side effects Hypnic jerks basically every time I was trying to fall asleep my body would jerk itself awake I ended up getting about 2 hours of sleep each night but because the nausea was controlled I pushed through until one night I’m sure this was because I was so sleep deprived I cried for hours and hours my partner said he’s never seen me like this before, honestly it felt like my body was failing me. Medication that made me feel human was also making me struggle mentally so I ended up reducing back to 1/2 a tablet which again worked wonders for about a week I actually felt happy, could clean the house, could even go shopping but then I started to get weird heart palpitations went to the doctors and did an ECG everything looks good but they recommended me stopping the unisom completely I know as soon as I stop the nausea and vomiting is going to get so much worse and any other recommendations for medications my doctor said they can suggest are all antihistamines which the doctor said will most likely have the same heart palpitation effect.
<!-- /verbatim -->

### 13. Knowing the risks of smoking, alcohol or drugs does not supply a workable way to stop or a safe-feeling way to disclose.

**Belongs in:** §§2, 4–6, 9; substance use, coping and access.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The restored words include panic, sadness, lack of excitement, body distress, regret and fear of not bonding or surviving birth.”

**What should change or be retained:** Add practical cessation requests, withdrawal, relapse, loss of coping, fear of child protection and guilt about exposure before recognition. Keep the accounts of easy cessation, improved functioning and sustained recovery. These are experiences and questions, not evidence that exposures are safe or unsafe at a stated dose.

[pbu17-0241] [pbu17-0262] [pbu22-0141] [pbu35-0261]

### 14. Pregnancy symptoms can remove basic capacity despite repeated remedies, reassuring scans or a supposedly easier trimester.

**Belongs in:** §§2, 4, 6, 8–10.

**Relation:** confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The broken week-by-week script includes persistent pain, exhaustion, vomiting and incontinence, repeated remedies and disrupted sleep.”

**What should change or be retained:** Describe function and the interactions, not just symptom names: keeping down medicine, feeding oneself, sitting at work, moving, sleeping and caring for a toddler. Relief may create a new problem such as constipation or sedation. Some symptoms improve; no timeline or remedy is uniform.

<a id="pbu28-0372"></a>

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0372 -->
> At 13 weeks pregnant, my ultrasound tech commented that my uterus is very low and very close to my back making it difficult to get really good images. My OB then diagnosed me with multi-organ prolapse, saying that my cervix was the worst. She instructed me to lighten my activity and not do any heavy lifting. At 14 weeks, I could not urinate for almost 12 hours, so I called my OB who advised I go immediately to the ER (I was out of town 8 hours away). At the ER, they did blood work and a bladder ultrasound then pushed 1000mL. They drained with a temporary catheter then let me sit to see if I could pee. An ultrasound tech came in to check on baby (looked good) and take a bunch of images for the on call OB to review. Twenty or so minutes after the first cath, I was in excruciating pain. I tried multiple times to pee, always failing, and called them multiple times telling them how much pain I was in. They brought Pyridium first thinking it was bladder spasms and then morphine a while later. Neither of these were any relief. I was shaking in bed from the pain. They finally did another bladder scan and catheter to drain almost 900 mL of fluid. Immediate relief!! The OB on call came in and diagnosed me with an incarcerated uterus showing on the monitor where my organs should be and where they were actually sitting. She tried manual manipulation in two different positions with and without a foley balloon then had me in an all fours downward dog position for approximately an hour. She came back to check me and said that there was still a lot of pressure where it shouldn’t be and wanted me to follow up with my OB as soon as I was back home but to call her directly if anything came up while I was still in the area.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0373"></a>
**[pbu28-0373]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0373 -->
> I have an appointment in approximately 36 hours with my regular OB office. Has anyone experienced all of this combined before - multi organ prolapse and incarcerated uterus? I’m terrified they won’t be able to manipulate my uterus back to where it should be, and I’ll end up needing surgery while pregnant. I am also so tired of this painful catheter already, and it’s only been two days since I got it. My husband is also on pins and needles because he’s on a work trip missing all of this and scared from a distance.
<!-- /verbatim -->

### 15. Parents need to understand changing birth and pain-relief choices, including failed or delayed relief and choices in both directions.

**Belongs in:** §4 birth preferences and consent; §§6, 10.

**Relation:** confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “it is not evidence for one preferred birth method.”

**What should change or be retained:** Keep successful and difficult induction, epidural, caesarean, VBAC and home-birth accounts side by side. Preparation should cover what a changed plan feels like and what the local service actually offers. Some want stronger intervention or surgery; others want to avoid it. No account establishes causation by a method.

### 16. For some assault survivors, the feared problem is touch, exposure or an unfamiliar man nearby, even if pain is controlled.

**Belongs in:** §4 consent; §§5–6, 9–10.

**Relation:** extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The issue is more specific than plans changing”

**What should change or be retained:** Make the sensory and overnight environment explicit: hands versus tools, panic during exams, ward policies, support-person availability, filming and sharing without consent. An epidural or planned section does not automatically answer the trigger. Keep the clinician who stops the examination immediately.

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0104 -->
> Sometimes I get horny for my parter and I feel desire, but when we have sex, i feel.. nothing. like it physically feels as if the nerves are no longer there. If i do happen to O, it feels different and i don’t have that mental connection or full body release. I’m so sad. I also cry during or after sex now. In my first trimester we still had decent sex despite how exhausted or nauseous i was. Then it started feeling different - my vagina does not feel the same to me. I can’t feel myself tightening or doing anything. I’m worried that it won’t be better post partum because i have psychosomatic anxiety that manifests as blockages around fear and i have terrible fear about giving birth. During penetration sometimes all i can suddenly think about is the fact that something is going to be exiting my body through there and people will be watching. I can’t help but to imagine the pain and panic i’ll be going through. So post birth, i’ll be so scared to even interact with my body sexually after such an event. I’m afraid all i’ll be reminded of is birth. Does it ever get better??
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0281"></a>

> Recent ultrasound which was on Friday Sept. 4th. (2 weeks later) She asks if I have any symptoms I tell her oh yeah! But that I’m managing. I told her I’m concerned about some light brown discharge I’ve never seen before and then she asked if it followed intercourse. I told her no and then she just gave me a blank stare for a couple seconds then proceeded to tell me “well idk why that’s happening then” again very insensitive to someone who’s had 2 miscarriages. Maybe I’m just being too sensitive idk. So we get to the ultrasound again and she touches my clit again and I jump not expecting it again. Is this normal??. Now she was inside looking for the baby and my eyes filled with happy tears as I saw the little flickering. My baby had a heartbeat! She told me “well we gotta measure it to see if it’s a good one” it was at 163 bpm which gave me such relief however she told me my uterus is kinda foggy. I asked her what could cause this? she told me “I don’t know but it’s gonna be hard finding anything now.” She then told me baby is like the size of a grain of rice. But my husband and I noticed it was bigger this time. I told her “that’s what you said last time though” then she told me “well yeah babies stay small and I’m putting you back at 6 weeks” then she manually entered her own guess for the baby’s gestational age. My question is are these all normal things? My husband and I don’t have a good feeling and are in the process of switching providers. I feel like I need to make a complaint, but I don’t wanna be one of those people :/

**Further supporting IDs** (including records quoted under another pattern):

[pbu17-0359]

### 17. A flexible plan, a healthy baby or an easy physical recovery can coexist with trauma, missing memories and a changed view of future children.

**Belongs in:** §§2, 4–6, 9–10.

**Relation:** confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “A flexible plan helps some people, but the earlier flexibility conclusion cannot explain every outcome or place responsibility for traumatic care on the woman.”

**What should change or be retained:** Describe the separate mechanisms: emergency threat, pain left untreated, lack of updates, unreported procedures, memory gaps, separation, privacy and aftercare delays. Include requests for notes, debriefs, an advocate and treatment, and the possibility that a severe event has a positive emotional aftermath.

<a id="pbu22-0005"></a>
**[pbu22-0005]**

<a id="pbu22-0062"></a>

> After a misdiagnosis of a missed miscarriage and two rounds of the abortion pill failing- our little man held on and fought so hard and was determined to be part of our family!  We welcomed him on 9/12/26
> 
> I didn’t get to enjoy my pregnancy because I was terrified of losing him. I feel like the whole experience was stripped from me. Why am I feeling so down?
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0098"></a>
**[pbu22-0098]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0098 -->
> My experience with the hospital during labour triggered significant mental health issues, including severe depression, anxiety and PTSD. I was eventually diagnosed with these conditions, and continue to receive therapy and medication with the support of a psychologist and a psychiatrist. My marriage nearly fell apart, and I honestly considered suicide. I am still working on my recovery, and I feel like I've made a lot of progress, but I still have lots more to go. I am still prone to flashbacks and I have a list of triggers to avoid. I am lucky to have support, but I do feel sad about the cost. All my treatment has had to be privately funded and this is NOT cheap!! We have had to spend thousands of pounds, and this is a lot for our family. The trauma has changed me drastically and while I am glad to be alive, I do miss the old me and grieve the wife and mother I wanted to be but was incapable of being. I wish everyone in the UK understood that behind headlines of poor maternity services are countless real lives being destroyed. To add insult to injury, I asked for a hospital debrief in April, at 7 months post partum, and was offered an appointment in October! I am hopeful it will help my recovery, although I am aware of the possibility it might make my mental health worse.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu22-0099"></a>
**[pbu22-0099]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu22-0099 -->
> Was hospitalized 2 weeks PP due to breast abscess and mastitis. The overnight nurse put in my IV wrong and inspite of me complaining of severe pain, she pushed fluids and antibiotics through the same IV the whole night and my arm was swollen big by morning, I cried the whole night due to pain. I am so terrified of hospitals now, scared to even visit my obgyn for the 4 week checkup. I keep thinking what if a kid or someone older has a nurse like her, I cry all the time thinking what if my kids or husband or parents experience this.
> 
> My baby is not gaining weight, I breast feed and also supplement with formula. She did gain her birth weight by her 1 week appointment but nothing much after that. Likely my supply tanked due to infection and I feel like a failed mom. I had like one thing to do and could not do even that properly.
<!-- /verbatim -->

**[pbu22-0108]**

I felt personally responsible for her rough first week in the world, refusing to talk about it because I didn't want anyone to see me cry. The week apart made it so I couldn't feed her myself and subsequent attempts were met with failure.
<!-- /verbatim -->

<a id="pbu28-0091"></a>
**[pbu28-0091]**

He said he couldn't comment on why the doctor/reg that night did not do it. I was crying and very visibly distressed to find out that it was 4th degree and that the cut I thought had been carried out had not been.

**[pbu28-0117]**

<!-- verbatim:pbu28-0117 -->

I was in theatre so long they had to give him a bottle, and now I'm struggling to have him latch as he's used to the bottles. **[pbu28-0118]**

Sorry if this is the least coherent post ever. My mind cannot switch off with the knowledge of what happened. I put my trust into my midwives and that man and I have come out of it with the worst possible tear and lasting consequences.

My blood pressure kept dropping and heart rate kept increasing from the pain, resulting in some scary moments for my family. It wasn’t until a night nurse saw me jumping from contractions while sedated by morphine and advocated for me that the doctors took me seriously. And 5 days after first going in with severe pain, I was induced and put on a better monitor where they could read that I had been having consistent contractions and likely had been the entire time, I was immediately given an epidural and as soon as my body relaxed I began dilating,

I remember when my son came out I was in complete shock. I always envisioned this moment of pure love like an explosion, I honestly didn’t feel anything. I felt scared that he was going to die. I thought I might too but that didn’t worry me as much.
